9	- **A reveal under a tear/wipe must be hidden until the tear.** Size the revealed photo exactly like the card. Add a quick decaying shake on the impact.

"""Jennifer-style stills rendered with the skill's own engine (scripts2/jstyle2.py).
  python jen_stills.py tools
"""
import os, sys
import numpy as np, cv2
from PIL import Image
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser('~/.claude/skills/jennifer-musselman-style/scripts2'))
import jstyle2 as J

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, 'stills')


def finish(img, seed=0, vig=0.30):
    """graphics finish from the skill: grain + vignette"""
    img = J.grain(img, 5.0, seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:J.H, 0:J.W].astype(np.float32)
    r = np.sqrt(((xx - J.W / 2) / (J.W * 0.62)) ** 2 + ((yy - J.H / 2) / (J.H * 0.55)) ** 2)
    img = img * (1 - vig * np.clip(r - 0.35, 0, 1) ** 1.4)[..., None]
    return np.clip(img, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)


def save(img, name):
    p = os.path.join(OUT, name + '.png')
    Image.fromarray(finish(img)).save(p)
    print('rendered', p)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 07  "Because AI isn't just changing tools"

 "command": "cat > ~/jl-trailer/tools/ytsb.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"fetch YouTube storyboard sprites and build a labelled contact sheet: ytsb.py id 'spec' out.jpg\"\"\"\nimport sys, io, urllib.request, math\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw\nvid, spec, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]\nparts = spec.split('|'); base = parts[0]; levels = parts[1:]\nL = len(levels) - 1\nwhile L > 0 and int(levels[L].split('#')[0]) > 170: L -= 1      # biggest level with tiles <= 170 px\nw, h, cnt, cols, rows, iv, name, sigh = levels[L].split('#')\nw, h, cnt, cols, rows, iv = int(w), int(h), int(cnt), int(cols), int(rows), int(iv)\nper = cols * rows; nsheets = math.ceil(cnt / per)\ntiles = []\nfor s in range(nsheets):\n    u = base.replace('$L', str(L)).replace('$N', name.replace('$M', str(s))) + '&sigh=' + sigh\n    im = Image.open(io.BytesIO(urllib.request.urlopen(u).read())).convert('RGB')\n    for k in range(per):\n        if len(tiles) >= cnt: break\n        x, y = (k % cols) * w, (k // cols) * h\n        if y + h > im.height: break\n        tiles.append(im.crop((x, y, x + w, y + h)))\nC = 10; tw, th = 192, round(192 * h / w)\nS = Image.new('RGB', (C * tw, math.ceil(len(tiles) / C) * (th + 14)), (30, 30, 30)); d = ImageDraw.Draw(S)\nfor i, t in enumerate(tiles):\n    x, y = (i % C) * tw, (i // C) * (th + 14)\n    S.paste(t.resize((tw, th)), (x, y + 14))\n    sec = i * iv / 1000 if iv else i\n    d.text((x + 3, y + 1), f'{int(sec)//60}:{sec%60:04.1f}', fill=(255, 220, 0))\nS.save(out, q

       - side_shade(d, side, frac=0.30, edge=0.37), bottom_shade

       - gbox
       - t1_lines(d, lines, x, base_y, px=120, align, key=(), leading=0.94): Trailer-1 big serif with key words on the gradient box
       - neon(d, word, cx, cy, px=84, lead=''): straight neon box plus lead line
       - text_behind(d, plate, alpha, draw): Dime roto
       - hairline_grid(d, x0, y0, x1, y1, col=(205,215,225), op=0.42): Twiml grid on black
       - card(d, img, x0, y0, w, h, …)
       - place_rgba(d, rgba, cx, cy, h|w, shadow, glow)
       - invert_block
